915 entries, lamps to landin-docs-rewrite. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lamps**: Run the fixed set of task-independent questions (the lamps) that light the regions a task-as-posed leaves dark - who decides and what they must find, what they default to, who argues against us, where the biggest expected loss is, the decision we lose in the decider's voice, what proves each finding, what paragraph the decider carries out, what here is not true, what the clock does, and the five questions the user did not ask. Trigger at four moments for any deliverable a person will decide on (a landing page, an offer, a proposal, a release, a client deliverable, a plan) - T0 intake ("we're… ([Traviseric/best-practices](https://github.com/Traviseric/best-practices/tree/HEAD/skills/lamps))
- **lampson-system-design**: Design systems using Butler Lampson's principles of abstraction, interfaces, and practical wisdom. Emphasizes clean abstractions, security foundations, and time-tested design hints. Use when making architectural decisions, designing APIs, or building systems that must evolve over decades. ([aibot88/sec_skill_store](https://github.com/aibot88/sec_skill_store/tree/HEAD/skills/claudskills/lampson) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **lampson-system-design**: Design long-lived computer systems in Butler Lampson's style: write abstract state and actions first, keep lower layers replaceable, put correctness end-to-end, and optimize for repair, tail latency, and evolvability. Use when choosing interfaces, retry/replication/transaction strategies, control-plane vs data-plane splits, storage hardening, or security boundaries. Trigger keywords: abstraction, spec, end-to-end, idempotence, retry, OCC, snapshot isolation, commit record, failover, repair, capability, least common mechanism, control plane. ([sammyjoyce/sk1llz](https://github.com/sammyjoyce/sk1llz/tree/HEAD/domains/systems-architecture/lampson))
- **lampung-design-system**: Build any interface (plain HTML, Next.js or another web framework, Flutter) with Lampung Design System. Covers tokens, components, icons, motion, dark mode, and the rules that keep the result looking like the system. - **lander**: 帮助 Agent 识别 GitHub 项目的官方使用入口，并完成安装、运行、部署、导入与验证，适用于本地电脑、WSL2 和远程服务器。当用户要求让仓库可运行、可使用、可安装、可部署、可导入，或比较这些官方路径时使用。先判断目标和生命周期，再选择轻量或服务路线，并仅在出现秘密信息、公网入口、既有 Web 栈、脚本管理入口或长期运维时加载对应规则。 ([hajimi2024/lander](https://github.com/hajimi2024/lander/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **lander-copy**: Generate RMBC-structured landing page copy — mobile-first, conversion-optimized, with value-stack pricing and full page architecture. ([coleschaffer/copywritingskills-rmbc](https://github.com/coleschaffer/copywritingskills-rmbc/tree/HEAD/skills/lander-copy) · ★42 · 3 copies)
- **lander-empty-queue**: Act when the merge queue reads empty or frozen. Use to tell a real drain from a stall, and to pick the next useful work. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-empty-queue) · ★1)
- **lander-inventory**: Internal kit of flights:lander and flights:reviewer — lander-inventory.py lists what a diff left behind (LEFT, LITERAL, GONE rows) and computes the review's spawn PLAN. Not invoked directly — ask flights:lander or flights:reviewer. ([rezzminator/flights](https://github.com/rezzminator/flights/tree/HEAD/plugins/flights/skills/lander-inventory))
- **lander-judge-a-handed-over-branch**: Judge a branch handed over by another session or a dead lane. Use to decide whether its content already landed, and whether the tip has diverged. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-judge-a-handed-over-branch) · ★1)
- **lander-ledger-work**: File, close or reconcile a ledger item in docs/BACKLOG.md. Use when writing a banner, closing an item, or running the reconcile pass. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-ledger-work) · ★1)
- **lander-pr-content-hazards**: Check a pull request whose own content carries a hazard. Use when the diff touches an ADR, redacts a secret, is docs-only, or contains a glyph. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-pr-content-hazards) · ★1)
- **lander-reach-for-an-instrument**: Choose and read a git, gh or shell instrument during landing work. Use before trusting a count, a scan, an exit code, or a green check. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-reach-for-an-instrument) · ★1)
- **lander-relay-or-correct-a-claim**: Relay, correct or broadcast a claim to peers or the owner. Use before sending a caution, a correction, or a finding about someone else's work. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-relay-or-correct-a-claim) · ★1)
- **lander-resolve-a-conflict**: Resolve a merge conflict on a pull request you are landing. Use when a PR reads DIRTY or a merge is refused, including disarming before you resolve. ([MEFORORG/korus](https://github.com/MEFORORG/korus/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lander-resolve-a-conflict) · ★1)
- **lander-triage-a-red-check**: Triage a failed required check on a pull request you are landing.
